000118: // Launch migration entry point. First repair reliable full-return sequences,
000119: // then retain the old conservative fallback for any genuinely untagged rows
000120: // whose historical snapshots are too old to reconstruct safely.
000121: function _migrateSaleNoTags(){
000122:   let touched=0;
000123:   try{
000124:     (typeof allDBKeys==='function'?allDBKeys():[]).forEach(function(k){
000125:       (Array.isArray(DB[k])?DB[k]:[]).forEach(function(it){
000126:         if(!it||!Array.isArray(it.returnHistory)||!it.returnHistory.length)return;
000127:         let itemTouched=_repairSaleNoSequenceForItem(it);
000128:         const rd=it.resaleDateSold?String(it.resaleDateSold).slice(0,10):null;
000129:         const fullNos=it.returnHistory.filter(function(r){return r&&(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay')&&r.saleNo!=null;})
000130:           .map(function(r){return Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);});
000131:         const maxFullNo=fullNos.length?Math.max.apply(null,fullNos):1;
000132:         // If the item is currently fully returned, the latest full-return row
000133:         // already represents the current sale. Otherwise the live resale (if
000134:         // any) is the cycle after the latest completed full return.
000135:         const currentResaleNo=(it.isReturned&&maxFullNo>=2)?maxFullNo:Math.max(2,maxFullNo+1);
000136:         it.returnHistory.forEach(function(r){
000137:           if(!r||r.saleNo!=null)return;
000138:           const logged=r.loggedAt?String(r.loggedAt).slice(0,10):null;
000139:           if(rd&&logged&&logged>=rd){r.saleNo=currentResaleNo;itemTouched++;}
000140:           else {r.saleNo=1;itemTouched++;}
000141:         });
000142:         if(itemTouched){
000143:           touched+=itemTouched;
000144:           try{if(typeof calcGrossProfit==='function')it.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(it);}catch(e){}
000145:         }
000146:       });
000147:     });
000148:   }catch(e){console.warn('[RETRADE] Sale-N history repair failed',e);}
000149:   return touched;
000150: }
000151: function allDBKeys(){
000152:   const curFY=_currentFYStart();
000153:   const fySet=new Set([curFY-1, curFY, curFY+1]);
000154:   // Also include any FY that has data already
000155:   Object.keys(DB).forEach(function(k){
000156:     if(k==='trips'||k==='expenses'||k==='_userOwned'||k==='_taxYear')return;
000157:     if(!DB[k]||!DB[k].length)return;
000158:     const code=keyCode(k); const yr=keyYear(k);
000159:     if(!MONTHS.includes(code))return;
000160:     // Determine FY for this key
000161:     const mo=MONTHS.indexOf(code);
000162:     const fy=mo>=3?yr:yr-1;
000163:     fySet.add(fy);
000164:   });
000165:   const keys=[];
000166:   Array.from(fySet).sort(function(a,b){return a-b;}).forEach(function(fy){
000167:     _fyKeys(fy).forEach(function(k){if(!keys.includes(k))keys.push(k);});
000168:   });
000169:   return keys;
000170: }
000171: function _findItemRecordById(id){
000172:   let found=null;
000173:   allDBKeys().some(function(k){
000174:     const idx=(DB[k]||[]).findIndex(function(i){return i.id===id;});
000175:     if(idx<0)return false;
000176:     found={item:DB[k][idx],month:k,index:idx};
000177:     return true;
000178:   });
000179:   return found;
000180: }

000182: // Guarantees DB has the expected shape: an array for every allDBKeys() month,
000183: // plus DB.trips and DB.expenses. Idempotent — safe to call anywhere. Fixes
000184: // the "DB[m] is undefined" crash for brand-new users whose loadFromSupabase
000185: // returned zero rows and therefore never populated any month keys.
000186: function _ensureDBShape(){
000187:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
000188:     if(!Array.isArray(DB[k])) DB[k]=[];
000189:   });
000190:   if(!Array.isArray(DB.trips)) DB.trips=[];
000191:   if(!Array.isArray(DB.expenses)) DB.expenses=[];

000194: // Patch A — Item state migration. One-time pass: every item must have a
000195: // canonical `state` field of 'sourced' | 'listed' | 'sold' | 'returned'.
000196: // State is purely for filtering/display in this patch — financial logic
000197: // continues to read existing fields (sold/isReturned/resaleSalePrice etc).
000198: // Derivation rules (in order):
000199: //   active return in returnHistory[] (no resale yet)         → 'returned'
000200: //   has dateSold or resaleSalePrice (and not returned)       → 'sold'
000201: //   otherwise                                                → 'listed'
000202: // Stock items (state='stock') are pre-listing and will only ever exist if
000203: // they were created via the Stock Quick Add flow — never produced by this
000204: // migration (no existing field can imply pre-listing intent).
000205: //
000206: // ALSO: backfill dateSourced for items that don't have it. Anything created
000207: // via the old List Quick Add flow gets dateSourced=dateListed (effectively
000208: // "sourced and listed same day" → 0d time-to-list). Falls back to dateSold
000209: // for ancient entries with neither, ensuring the field is never undefined.
000210: // This matters for the Stock page's "Avg time to list" KPI which would
000211: // otherwise have to skip un-sourced items entirely.
000212: function _migrateItemStates(){
000213:   const _VALID_STATES=['sourced','listed','sold','returned'];
000214:   let touchedState=0, touchedSourced=0, reconciled=0;
000215:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
000216:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
000217:       // E7: remap legacy 'stock' → 'sourced'. Items saved before E6 renamed the
000218:       // pre-listing state carry 'stock', which the DB CHECK constraint rejects.
000219:       // 'stock' is truthy so the !i.state guard below would silently skip it.
000220:       if(i.state==='stock'){
000221:         i.state='sourced';
000222:         touchedState++;
000223:       }
000224:       // Catch null/undefined/empty/any other unrecognised value
000225:       if(!i.state || !_VALID_STATES.includes(i.state)){
000226:         const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
000227:         const _activeReturn=i.isReturned===true || (_hadFullReturn && !i.resaleSalePrice);
000228:         if(_activeReturn){ i.state='returned'; }
000229:         else if(i.dateSold || i.resaleSalePrice){ i.state='sold'; }
000230:         else { i.state='listed'; }
000231:         touchedState++;
000232:       }
000233:       // v2.09.4 — Reconcile ghost states left by pre-v2.09.2 duplicates. An item
000234:       // with state='sold' but no dateSold AND no resaleSalePrice AND not
000235:       // returned isn't actually sold — the source item was, the duplicate
000236:       // inherited the state but had its sale fields cleared. Stock Room shows
000237:       // these correctly as listed (its filter ignores state), but the account
000238:       // page and other state-driven UIs read the wrong badge. Correct here.
000239:       // Same pattern for state='returned' without an isReturned/return record.
000240:       if(i.state==='sold' && !i.dateSold && !i.resaleSalePrice && !i.isReturned){
000241:         i.state='listed';
000242:         reconciled++;
000243:       } else if(i.state==='returned' && !i.isReturned){
000244:         const _hadFullReturn=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r){return r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay';});
000245:         if(!_hadFullReturn){
000246:           // No supporting evidence — treat as active. If dateSold survived,
000247:           // it was truly sold before the return got cleared; otherwise listed.
000248:           i.state=(i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)?'sold':'listed';
000249:           reconciled++;
000250:         }
000251:       }
000252:       if(!i.dateSourced){
000253:         i.dateSourced = i.dateListed || i.dateSold || null;
000254:         if(i.dateSourced) touchedSourced++;
000255:       }
000256:     });
000257:   });
000258:   if(reconciled>0)console.info('[RETRADE] v2.09.4 state reconcile: fixed '+reconciled+' ghost-sold/returned item'+(reconciled!==1?'s':''));
000259:   return reconciled;
000260: }

000262: // On load: migrate bare keys ('APR') → year-suffixed ('APR-26') using dateListed/dateSold
000263: // to infer the correct year. Runs once after loadFromSupabase.
000264: function _migrateBareDBKeys(){
000265:   const curFY=_currentFYStart();
000266:   MONTHS.forEach(function(code){
000267:     if(!DB[code]||!DB[code].length)return;
000268:     const items=DB[code];
000269:     // Group items by their actual year — infer from dateListed or dateSold
000270:     const byYear={};
000271:     items.forEach(function(i){
000272:       const dateStr=i.dateListed||i.dateSold||null;
000273:       let yr=curFY; // fallback: assume current tax year context
000274:       if(dateStr){
000275:         const d=new Date(dateStr);
000276:         if(!isNaN(d)) yr=d.getFullYear();
000277:       } else {
000278:         // No date: use FY logic — Jan/Feb/Mar belong to prev year's tail
000279:         const mo=MONTHS.indexOf(code);
000280:         yr = mo>=3 ? curFY : curFY+1; // APR-DEC → curFY; JAN-MAR → curFY+1 (next calendar yr)
000281:         // But only if we're actually past APR — if it's early year, shift back
000282:         if(mo<3) yr=curFY; // conservative: keep in current tracked year
000283:       }
000284:       const yy=String(yr).slice(-2);
000285:       const key=code+'-'+yy;
000286:       if(!byYear[key])byYear[key]=[];
000287:       byYear[key].push(i);

002932: // Production schema handshake. The launch migration writes this value only

002934: async function _verifySchemaVersion(){
002935:   if(ALLOW_SCHEMA_FALLBACK)return true;
002936:   const res=await _sbCall(()=>_sb.from('retrade_meta').select('value').eq('key','schema_version').single());
002937:   if(res.error)throw new Error('Database migration check failed. Run the current RETRADE Supabase migration before using this build. '+res.error.message);
002938:   if(!res.data||res.data.value!==RETRADE_SCHEMA_VERSION){
002939:     throw new Error('Database schema version mismatch. App expects '+RETRADE_SCHEMA_VERSION+' but Supabase reports '+((res.data&&res.data.value)||'none')+'.');
002940:   }
002941:   return true;
002942: }

003913: // SINGLE choke point: _captureActivity() runs at the top of every real saveDB,
003914: // diffs each item's committed status against a shadow taken at the previous
003915: // capture, and logs each STATE TRANSITION with the item's prior snapshot. Being
003916: // driven by committed state, it catches modal-driven mutations too, and it
003917: // never edits a mutation function or the money/tax engine. Undo restores a
003918: // snapshot and only the LATEST change on an item is undoable. Log rows ride the
003919: // same diff + outbox durability as every other entity.
003920: // ============================================================

003923: function _newId(prefix){
003924:   let token='';
003925:   try{ token=(globalThis.crypto&&typeof globalThis.crypto.randomUUID==='function')?globalThis.crypto.randomUUID():''; }catch(e){}
003926:   if(!token) token=Date.now().toString(36)+'_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,11);
003927:   return (prefix?prefix+'_':'')+token;
003928: }
003929: function _uuidLog(){ return _newId('log'); }
003930: // Money-free canonical status label (mirrors the list-row status deriver).
003931: function _actStatus(i){
003932:   if(!i) return 'gone';
003933:   if(i.scrappedAt){ if(i.scrapReason==='supplier_return')return 'returned_supplier'; return (i.scrapReason==='donated')?'donated':'scrapped'; }
003934:   if(i.isReturned) return 'returned';
003935:   if(i.resaleSalePrice) return 'resold';
003936:   if(i.dateSold) return 'sold';
003937:   if(i.state==='sourced') return 'sourced';
003938:   return 'listed';
003939: }
003940: const _ACT_LABEL = { listed:'Listed', sold:'Marked sold', returned:'Returned', resold:'Re-sold', scrapped:'Scrapped', donated:'Donated', returned_supplier:'Returned to supplier', sourced:'Moved to unlisted', deleted:'Deleted' };
003941: function _findItemMonth(id){ const ks=allDBKeys(); for(const m of ks){ if((DB[m]||[]).some(x=>x.id===id)) return m; } return null; }
003942: function _activityAfterSummary(beforeObj,afterObj,toState){
003943:   try{
003944:     const i=afterObj||{};
003945:     if(toState==='sold'||toState==='resold'){
003946:       const saleNo=(typeof _currentResaleSaleNo==='function'&&i.resaleSalePrice)?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):1;
003947:       const snap=(typeof _saleCycleSnapshot==='function')?_saleCycleSnapshot(i,saleNo):null;
003948:       const price=snap?Number(snap.price)||0:Number(i.resaleSalePrice||i.salePrice)||0;
003949:       const platform=snap&&snap.platformId?(typeof _platformLabel==='function'?_platformLabel(snap.platformId):snap.platformId):'';
003950:       return 'Sale '+saleNo+' · '+(typeof fmt==='function'?fmt(price):('£'+price.toFixed(2)))+(platform?' · '+platform:'');
003951:     }
003952:     if(toState==='returned'){
003953:       const r=(i.returnHistory||[]).slice().reverse().find(function(x){return x&&(x.type==='full_seller'||x.type==='full_ebay');});
003954:       if(r){
003955:         const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1),refund=Math.max(0,Number(r.refundAmount)||0);
003956:         return 'Sale '+saleNo+' returned'+(refund?(' · refund '+(typeof fmt==='function'?fmt(refund):('£'+refund.toFixed(2)))):'');
003957:       }
003958:       return 'Item returned to stock';
003959:     }
003960:     if(toState==='listed'){
003961:       if(beforeObj&&beforeObj.isReturned)return 'Returned stock relisted';
003962:       return i.dateListed?('Listed '+i.dateListed):'Listed for sale';
003963:     }
003964:     if(toState==='sourced')return i.dateSourced?('Unlisted stock · sourced '+i.dateSourced):'Moved to unlisted stock';
003965:     if(toState==='returned_supplier'){
003966:       const refund=Math.max(0,Number(i.supplierRefund)||0);
003967:       return 'Returned to supplier'+(refund?(' · refund '+(typeof fmt==='function'?fmt(refund):('£'+refund.toFixed(2)))):'');
003968:     }
003969:     if(toState==='scrapped'||toState==='donated')return i.scrapNotes||(_ACT_LABEL[toState]||toState);

005707: // when there's no sales history yet.
005708: function _avgPromoRate(){

005714:     });

005717:   return rates.reduce(function(s,r){return s+r;},0)/rates.length;
005718: }
005719: // v2.21.12 — unsold-item net estimate. LISTED items (real asking price already
005720: // set + real postage/shipping/packaging known) use the realistic estimate with
005721: // their actual charges (calcEstProfit). SOURCED items (only a guess) use a
005722: // conservative estimate: real fee model on the est price + estimated promo drag
005723: // + MEDIAN fulfilment drag. Always modest — never assumes zero fees/postage/promo.
005724: function _estPotentialGross(i){
005725:   if(i.salePrice&&!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned){
005726:     const e=calcEstGrossProfit(i);
005727:     if(e!=null)return e;
005728:   }

005730:   if(est<=0)return null;
005731:   const bpf=calcPlatformBPF(i,est,0,_itemPlatform(i));
005732:   const promo=est*_avgPromoRate()*1.20;
005733:   return +(est-bpf-promo-(i.costPrice||0)-calcPartsCost(i)-_medianFulfilmentDrag()).toFixed(2);
005734: }
005735: function _estPotentialNet(i){

005737:   if(gross==null)return null;
005738:   return +(gross-_estimatedPartnerCutFromProfit(i,gross)).toFixed(2);

006524: function highlightMatch(text,q){
006525:   if(!text||!q.trim())return esc(text||'');
006526:   const terms=q.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
006527:   let result=esc(text);
006528:   terms.forEach(t=>{
006529:     const escaped=t.replace(/[-\/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g,'\\$&');
006530:     const re=new RegExp('('+escaped+')','gi');
006531:     result=result.replace(re,'<mark>$1</mark>');
006532:   });
006533:   return result;
006534: }

008026: function _renderRunPage(run){
008027:   const page=document.getElementById('p-item');
008028:   if(!page)return;
008029:   const items=_runItems(run.id);
008030:   const runTrips=(DB.trips||[]).filter(function(t){return t.sourcingRunId===run.id;});
008031:   const runExps=(DB.expenses||[]).filter(function(e){return e.sourcingRunId===run.id;});
008032: 
008033:   // ── Costs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
008034:   const itemCost=items.reduce(function(s,i){return s+(i.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(i);},0);
008035:   const tripCost=runTrips.reduce(function(s,t){
008036:     return s+(t.mileage||0)*(t.ratePerMile||0.45)+((Array.isArray(t.expenses)?t.expenses:[]).reduce(function(a,e){return a+(e.amount||0);},0));
008037:   },0);
008038:   const expCost=runExps.reduce(function(s,e){return s+(e.amount||0);},0);
008039:   const totalSpent=itemCost+tripCost+expCost;
008040:   const totalMiles=runTrips.reduce(function(s,t){return s+(t.mileage||0);},0);
008041:

011999: function showCategoryItems(cat,_fromBack){
012000:   let items=[];
012001:   allDBKeys().forEach(k=>{(DB[k]||[]).forEach(i=>{
012002:     if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return;
012003:     if((i.category||'Other')===cat)items.push({...i,month:k});
012004:   });});
012005:   const sold=items.filter(i=>(i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)&&!i.isReturned);
012006:   const active=items.filter(i=>!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt);
012007:   items.sort((a,b)=>{
012008:     const statusOrder=i=>{
012009:       if(!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt)return 0;
012010:       if((i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)&&!i.isReturned)return 1;
012011:       return 2;
012012:     };
012013:     const so=statusOrder(a)-statusOrder(b);
012014:     if(so!==0)return so;
012015:     if(statusOrder(a)===0){
012016:       const _today=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
012017:       const dA=a.dateListed?daysBetween(a.dateListed,_today):0;
012018:       const dB=b.dateListed?daysBetween(b.dateListed,_today):0;
012019:       return dB-dA;
012020:     }
012021:     const dateA=(a.resaleDateSold||a.dateSold||'');
012022:     const dateB=(b.resaleDateSold||b.dateSold||'');
012023:     return dateB.localeCompare(dateA);
012024:   });
012025:   const totalProfit=sold.reduce((s,i)=>s+calcNetProfit(i),0);
012026:   const soldWithDate=sold.filter(i=>calcDaysToSell(i)!==null);
012027:   const avgDays=soldWithDate.length?Math.round(soldWithDate.reduce((s,i)=>s+calcDaysToSell(i),0)/soldWithDate.length):null;
012028:   const backBtn=!_fromBack?`<button onclick="closePanel();showAllCategoriesModal(window.__summaryByCat)" style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--accent);font-size:13px;font-weight:600;padding:0 0 14px;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent">← All categories</button>`:'';
012029:   const html=`
012030:     ${backBtn}
012031:     <div style="margin-bottom:16px;background:var(--surface2);border-radius:10px;padding:14px">
012032:       <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px">${esc(cat)}</div>
012033:       <div style="display:flex;gap:16px;font-size:13px;color:var(--text-secondary);flex-wrap:wrap">
012034:         <span>${items.length} total</span>
012035:         <span style="color:${totalProfit>=0?'var(--green)':'var(--red)'}">${sold.length} sold · ${fmt(totalProfit)} profit</span>
012036:         <span style="color:var(--muted)">${active.length} active</span>
012037:         ${avgDays!==null?`<span style="color:var(--text-secondary)">avg ${avgDays}d to sell</span>`:''}
012038:       </div>
012039:     </div>
012040:     ${items.map(i=>{
012041:       const _today=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
012042:       const status=i.scrappedAt?(i.scrapReason==='donated'?'donated':'scrapped'):i.isReturned?'returned':i.resaleSalePrice?'resold':i.dateSold?'sold':i.condition==='spares'?'spares':'listed';

012044:       const daysToSell=!i.isReturned&&(i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)?calcDaysToSell(i):null;
012045:       const _eld=_effectiveListDate(i);
012046:       const daysListed=!i.dateSold&&!i.isReturned&&!i.resaleSalePrice&&!i.scrappedAt&&_eld?daysBetween(_eld,_today):null;

012202: function _snapshotSoldItems(){
012203:   // Dashboard drill-downs must use the exact same Sale-N transaction source as
012204:   // calcYearlyStats(). A physical item can therefore appear more than once in
012205:   // the selected period (Sale 1, Sale 2, Sale 3...) without earlier cycles
012206:   // disappearing when the item is resold again.
012207:   const range=_periodDateRange(SUMMARY_PERIOD);
012208:   return getSaleEventsInRange(range.from,range.to)
012209:     .filter(function(ev){return !ev.isReturnAdjustment;})
012210:     .map(function(ev){
012211:       const b=_saleBreakdown(ev),cyc=ev.snapshot||_saleCycleSnapshot(ev.item,ev.sale),saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(ev.sale)||1);
012212:       return Object.assign({},ev.item,{
012213:         month:ev.month,
012214:         _event:ev,
012215:         _saleNo:saleNo,
012216:         _saleDate:ev.saleDate||'',
012217:         _listedDate:(cyc&&cyc.listedDate)||null,
012218:         _salePrice:Number(b.salePrice)||0,
012219:         _postage:Number(b.postage)||0,
012220:         _profit:Number(b.netProfit)||0,
012221:         _roi:ev.roi,
012222:         _margin:ev.margin,
012223:         _days:ev.daysToSell
012224:       });
012225:     });
012226: }
012227: 
012228: function showAvgSaleBreakdown(){
012229:   const items=_snapshotSoldItems();

012231:   const prices=items.map(function(i){return Number(i._salePrice)||0;});
012232:   const avg=prices.reduce(function(s,v){return s+v;},0)/prices.length;
012233:   const periodLabel={'7d':'Last 7 days','30d':'Last 30 days','60d':'Last 60 days','90d':'Last 90 days','current_fy':'Current FY','prev_fy':'Previous FY'}[SUMMARY_PERIOD]||SUMMARY_PERIOD;
012234:   const sorted=items.slice().sort(function(a,b){return (Number(b._salePrice)||0)-(Number(a._salePrice)||0);});
012235:   const header=`<div style="margin-bottom:16px;background:var(--surface2);border-radius:10px;padding:14px">

015033: function selectAllMonth(){
015034:   // Select all items visible in current filter
015035:   // Session B: filter set is now sold | all | returned, driven by sale events.
015036:   const events=getSaleEventsInMonth(SELECTED_MONTH);
015037:   let visible=events;

015039:   else if(MONTH_FILTER==='returned')visible=events.filter(e=>e.isReturned);

015042:   else{visible.forEach(e=>SELECTED_ITEMS.add(e.item.id));}

015046: async function bulkAction(action){
015047:   if(action==='sold'){
015048:     const today=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
015049:     SELECTED_ITEMS.forEach(function(id){
015050:       const rec=_findItemRecordById(id);
015051:       const item=rec&&rec.item;
015052:       if(item&&!item.dateSold&&!item.resaleSalePrice){
015053:         item.dateSold=today;
015054:         item.isReturned=false;
015055:         item.state='sold';
015056:         if(!item.soldOnPlatform)item.soldOnPlatform=_itemPlatform(item);
015057:         item.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(item);
015058:       }
015059:     });
015060:     saveDB();
015061:     SELECTED_ITEMS.clear();SELECTION_MODE=false;
015062:     toast('Marked items as sold');
015063:     renderMonth();
015064:   }else if(action==='delete'){
015065:     const ok=await showConfirm('Delete '+SELECTED_ITEMS.size+' items?','This cannot be undone.',{icon:'delete',okLabel:'Delete all'});
015066:     if(!ok)return;
015067:     SELECTED_ITEMS.forEach(function(id){
015068:       const rec=_findItemRecordById(id);
015069:       if(rec){
015070:         const snap=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rec.item));
015071:         _pushActivity(snap.id,snap.item||'',_actStatus(snap),'deleted',snap,rec.month,'user-delete');
015072:         const fresh=_findItemRecordById(id);
015073:         if(fresh)DB[fresh.month].splice(fresh.index,1);
015074:       }
015075:     });

015244: // ── Job Lots (Launch Audit v1.01) ────────────────────────────────────────────
015245: // A Job Lot is a PHYSICAL inventory grouping, not a sale bundle. Child items
015246: // keep their own immutable purchase/return history and cost basis. While a lot
015247: // is building/listed, active members are hidden from the ordinary item queues so
015248: // one physical unit never appears to have two operational owners.
015249: function _jobLotById(id){return (_jobLots||[]).find(function(l){return l&&l.id===id;})||null;}
015250: function _activeJobLots(){return (_jobLots||[]).filter(function(l){return l&&(l.status==='building'||l.status==='listed');});}

015253:   return (_jobLotItems||[]).find(function(m){return m&&m.itemId===itemId&&!m.removedAt&&activeIds.has(m.jobLotId);})||null;
015254: }

015257:     .sort(function(a,b){return String(b.addedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.addedAt||''));})[0]||null;
015258: }

015346: function _canAddToJobLot(i){
015347:   if(!i||i.scrappedAt||_activeJobLotMembership(i.id))return false;
015348:   if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return false;
015349:   // Physical unit must be in hand: unsold first-sale stock OR a fully returned
015350:   // unit awaiting a second decision. A completed resale is not in inventory.
015351:   if(i.isReturned)return true;
015352:   if(i.resaleSalePrice)return false;
015353:   return !i.dateSold;
015354: }

015634: // A bundle = several items sold to ONE buyer in ONE order. Sale 1 and Sale 2
015635: // have independent bundle identities so a later resale can never rewrite the
015636: // grouping of an earlier historical sale.

015679: function _bundleItems(bid){
015680:   if(!bid)return [];
015681:   const out=[];
015682:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
015683:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
015684:       if(_bundleMemberCycle(i,bid))out.push(Object.assign({},i,{_month:k}));
015685:     });
015686:   });
015687:   return out.sort(function(a,b){return _bundleMemberSalePrice(b,bid)-_bundleMemberSalePrice(a,bid);});
015688: }
015689: // Split a total pro-rata by each item's listed (ask) price, using
015690: // largest-remainder so the allocated parts sum to the total EXACTLY (no penny
015691: // drift). Falls back to an even split when there are no listed prices.
015692: function _bundleSplit(total,weights){
015693:   const n=weights.length;
015694:   if(!n)return [];
015695:   const sum=weights.reduce(function(a,w){return a+(w||0);},0);
015696:   const cents=Math.round((total||0)*100);
015697:   const raw=weights.map(function(w){ return sum>0?(cents*(w||0)/sum):(cents/n); });
015698:   const base=raw.map(function(r){return Math.floor(r);});
015699:   let left=cents-base.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b;},0);
015700:   const order=raw.map(function(r,idx){return {idx:idx,frac:r-Math.floor(r)};})
015701:                  .sort(function(a,b){return b.frac-a.frac;});
015702:   for(let k=0;k<order.length&&left>0;k++){base[order[k].idx]++;left--;}
015703:   return base.map(function(c){return +(c/100).toFixed(2);});
015704: }

015711:     cost+=(i.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(i);
015712:     shipping+=_bundleMemberShipping(i,bid);
015713:     profit+=_bundleMemberProfit(i,bid);
015714:   });
015715:   const first=its[0]||{};
015716:   return {items:its,count:its.length,revenue:+revenue.toFixed(2),cost:+cost.toFixed(2),
015717:           shipping:+shipping.toFixed(2),profit:+profit.toFixed(2),
015718:           ref:_bundleMemberRef(first,bid),date:_bundleMemberSoldDate(first,bid),
015719:           total:_bundleMemberTotal(first,bid),

015865: // ============================================================
015866: // Combined v2.21.54 — RETROACTIVE “ADD TO A SALE”
015867: // Reconciles an orphan/legacy record into an existing sold order. This is a
015868: // repair tool rather than a normal sale path. It deliberately writes one
015869: // non-undoable Activity entry because restoring only the orphan would leave the
015870: // target bundle's reallocated sale prices inconsistent. A future grouped-undo
015871: // model can make this atomic across every member.
015872: // ============================================================
015873: function _findItemRef(id){const r=_findItemRecordById(id);return r?r.item:null;}
015874: function _linkTargetMembers(target){
015875:   const members=[],seen={};
015876:   function add(i){if(i&&!seen[i.id]){seen[i.id]=true;members.push(i);}}
015877:   if(target&&target.bundleId){
015878:     allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){(DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){if(i.bundleId===target.bundleId)add(i);});});
015879:   }
015880:   add(target);
015881:   return members;

015883: function _linkOrderTotal(target,members){

015920: function _applyIncludedReconcile(existing,orphan,total,dateSold,platform,target){

015923:   // mirrors normal bundle creation and avoids the old bug that weighted revenue
015924:   // by COST, which had no relationship to what the buyer paid.

016222: // ─── SELECTION / IN-LIST SEARCH TOTALS (v2.21.18) ─────────────────────────
016223: // ONE pure engine powers two features: (1) a live totals bar over the SELECTED
016224: // rows, (2) a live totals bar over the SEARCH-FILTERED rows. It accepts either
016225: // raw items (Stock Room) or sale-events (Sales History month view) and derives
016226: // every figure LIVE through the canonical calculators — nothing computed is
016227: // ever stored on the item (the never-store-financials invariant).
016228: function _selEntryIsEvent(e){
016229:   // Sale-events (from getSaleEventsInMonth) always carry a resolved .saleDate +
016230:   // an .item + a .sale/.isReturnAdjustment marker. Raw items never do.
016231:   return !!(e && e.item && e.saleDate!==undefined &&
016232:             (e.sale!==undefined || e.isReturnAdjustment!==undefined));
016233: }
016234: function _selectionTotals(entries){
016235:   let count=0,soldCount=0,unsoldCount=0,returnedCount=0;
016236:   let cost=0,revenue=0,realisedProfit=0,potentialProfit=0,estValue=0;
016237:   let soldRev=0,soldProfit=0,daysSum=0,daysCount=0;
016238:   (entries||[]).forEach(function(e){
016239:     if(!e)return;
016240:     count++;
016241:     if(_selEntryIsEvent(e)){
016242:       // Month sale-event: use the event's OWN leg figures so a selection/filter
016243:       // total reconciles exactly to calcMonthStatsBySale (event .profit already
016244:       // routes through _reconciledItemEventProfits).
016245:       const _br=_saleBreakdown(e);
016246:       const rev=e.isReturnAdjustment?(Number(e.salePrice)||0):((Number(_br.salePrice)||0)+(Number(_br.postage)||0));
016247:       revenue+=rev; realisedProfit+=(Number(_br.netProfit)||0);
016248:       if(e.isReturnAdjustment||e.isReturned){
016249:         returnedCount++;
016250:       } else {
016251:         soldCount++; soldRev+=rev; soldProfit+=(Number(_br.netProfit)||0);
016252:         if(e.daysToSell!=null){daysSum+=e.daysToSell;daysCount++;}
016253:       }
016254:     } else {
016255:       // Raw item.
016256:       const it=e;
016257:       const c=(it.costPrice||0)+calcPartsCost(it); cost+=c;
016258:       const isSold=!!(it.dateSold||it.resaleSalePrice)&&!it.isReturned;
016259:       if(isSold){
016260:         soldCount++;
016261:         const rev=calcItemRevenue(it), gp=calcGrossProfit(it);
016262:         revenue+=rev; realisedProfit+=(gp||0); soldRev+=rev; soldProfit+=(gp||0);
016263:         const d=calcDaysToSell(it); if(d!=null){daysSum+=d;daysCount++;}
016264:       } else if(it.isReturned){
016265:         returnedCount++;
016266:       } else {
016267:         unsoldCount++;
016268:         const pn=_estPotentialNet(it); if(pn!=null)potentialProfit+=pn;
016269:         estValue+=(it.salePrice||it.estSalePrice||0);
016270:       }
016271:     }
016272:   });
016273:   // Blended margin over the SOLD legs only (Σprofit / Σrevenue — never an avg of
016274:   // per-item margins). Headline realisedProfit nets returns; margin does not.
016275:   const margin = soldRev>0 ? +(soldProfit/soldRev*100).toFixed(1) : null;
016276:   const avgDays = daysCount>0 ? Math.round(daysSum/daysCount) : null;
016277:   return {count:count,soldCount:soldCount,unsoldCount:unsoldCount,returnedCount:returnedCount,
016278:           cost:+cost.toFixed(2),revenue:+revenue.toFixed(2),realisedProfit:+realisedProfit.toFixed(2),
016279:           potentialProfit:+potentialProfit.toFixed(2),estValue:+estValue.toFixed(2),
016280:           margin:margin,avgDays:avgDays};
016281: }

016825: // EXPENSES PAGE
016826: 
016827: // BUG-07: Per-trip cost engine with HMRC mid-trip tiering and per-trip expenses.
016828: // Returns an array (same length as input, same order) where each entry is:
016829: //   {id, mileageCost, expensesCost, totalCost, tier1Miles, tier2Miles,
016830: //    tier1Cost, tier2Cost, ratePerMile, isSplit}
016831: // Algorithm:
016832: //   1. Group trips by UK tax year (6 Apr - 5 Apr); the 10,000-mile HMRC
016833: //      tier-1 ceiling resets each tax year.
016834: //   2. Walk trips within each year in DATE order, maintaining cumulative
016835: //      milesUsed. For each trip:
016836: //        - all miles fit under 10k          → all at trip.ratePerMile
016837: //        - all miles already over 10k       → all at 0.25
016838: //        - mid-trip split                   → portion at trip.ratePerMile
016839: //                                             until 10k, remainder at 0.25
016840: //   3. Add per-trip expenses (sum of t.expenses[*].amount) on top.
016841: // Notes:
016842: //   - When a trip's stored ratePerMile differs from 0.45, tier-1 uses that
016843: //     stored rate (user override). Tier-2 always uses HMRC's 0.25.
016844: //   - Sums of all per-trip totalCost reconcile EXACTLY to the existing
016845: //     L5826-style year-aggregate formula (sum_year(min(M,10k)*0.45 +
016846: //     max(0,M-10k)*0.25)) when ratePerMile=0.45 across the board.
016847: // Launch hardening — Inventory is a high-frequency operational page. Keep a
016848: // local render boundary so one malformed legacy row cannot blank the entire
016849: // page or break mobile navigation. The underlying exception is still captured
016850: // by Diagnostics and logged to console; this only keeps the UI recoverable.
016851: function renderStock(){
016852:   const el=document.getElementById('p-stock');
016853:   try{
016854:     _renderStockCore();
016855:   }catch(err){
016856:     console.error('[RETRADE] renderStock failed',err);
016857:     try{ _diagRecord('render',err,{location:'Inventory'}); }catch(e){}
016858:     if(el){
016859:       const msg=(err&&err.message)?String(err.message):'Unknown rendering error';
016860:       el.innerHTML='<div class="page-header"><div><div class="page-title">Inventory</div><div class="page-subtitle">Listed, unlisted and returned stock.</div></div></div>'
016861:         +'<div class="section-card" style="border-color:color-mix(in srgb,var(--red) 35%,var(--border));">'
016862:         +'<div class="section-card-title">Inventory needs a refresh</div>'
016863:         +'<div class="section-card-desc">A stock record could not be rendered. Your data has not been deleted. Retry first; if it repeats, copy Diagnostics from Reports &amp; Data.</div>'
016864:         +'<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin:-6px 0 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere">'+esc(msg)+'</div>'

016993: // Launch hardening — backfill display-only cash audit rows for historical source
016994: // transactions created before linked cash events existed. These rows NEVER feed
016995: // calcCashSummary(); they only make the Cashflow ledger match the real source
016996: // transaction history. Deterministic ids keep this idempotent.
016997: function _backfillSystemCashEvents(){
016998:   let created=0;
016999:   const before=new Set(_cashLedger().map(function(x){return x.id;}));
017000:   (_accounts||[]).forEach(function(acct){
017001:     (acct.settlements||[]).forEach(function(tx){
017002:       if(!tx||!tx.paid)return;
017003:       const amount=Math.max(0,Number(tx.partnerAmount)||0);
017004:       if(!amount)return;
017005:       const id=_systemSettlementCashId(tx.id);
017006:       _recordSystemCashEvent(id,'partner_settlement',amount,tx.date||todayISO(),
017007:         'Partner / supplier settlement · '+(acct.name||'Partner')+' · '+((tx.items||[]).length)+' item'+((tx.items||[]).length===1?'':'s'));
017008:       if(!before.has(id)){before.add(id);created++;}
017009:     });
017010:   });
017011:   allDBKeys().forEach(function(k){
017012:     (DB[k]||[]).forEach(function(i){
017013:       if(!i||i.scrapReason!=='supplier_return')return;
017014:       const refund=Math.max(0,Number(i.supplierRefund)||0); if(!refund)return;
017015:       const id=_systemSupplierRefundCashId(i.id);
017016:       _recordSystemCashEvent(id,'supplier_refund',refund,(i.scrappedAt||todayISO()).slice(0,10),
017017:         'Supplier return refund · '+(i.item||'Item'));
017018:       if(!before.has(id)){before.add(id);created++;}
017019:     });
017020:   });
017021:   return created;
017022: }
017023: // Real cash overheads only. Standalone expenses + trip cash extras (parking,
017024: // entry). Tiered MILEAGE is deliberately excluded — it is a tax allowance, not
017025: // money that left the account, so it must not touch a cash balance.
017026: // Cash tied up in unsold stock = what Oskar has actually PAID for stock STILL ON
017027: // HAND. Two rules:
017028: //  1) On-hand only — mirror the Stock Room exactly: sourced, or not-yet
017029: //     sold/returned/resold. A sold item's cost is already inside realised profit,
017030: //     so counting it here too would double-count it (this was inflating the figure).
017031: //  2) Cost counts only if PAID: own stock (no partner account) is paid at
017032: //     purchase; partner stock counts only once actually settled (accountSettled),
017033: //     regardless of the account's payment terms. Unsettled partner stock ties up
017034: //     no cash. Parts always count (his cash). Amount = accountPaidAmount ?? costPrice.
017035: 
017036: // Profit is an accrual-style measure: supplier COGS and partner shares reduce
017037: // profit when earned/owed. Cash must only reduce when those liabilities are
017038: // actually PAID. Add unsettled sold-account debt back to the trading cash base.
017039: // This is the missing bridge between P&L and the real bank/cash/marketplace pots.
017040: 
017041: // Combined v2.21.55 — cash actually lost when inventory leaves the business.
017042: // Scrapped/donated/recycled stock recovers nothing; a supplier return recovers
017043: // supplierRefund. The item is already excluded from _cashStockTied(), so this
017044: // counter-entry prevents removing stock from falsely creating cash.
017045:

017221: // ── SCRAP / WRITE-OFF ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
017222: // A returned item Oskar won't relist gets a terminal 'scrapped' flag. Its buy
017223: // cost was already booked as COGS when the original sale it came back from was
017224: // recorded (SA103 Box 17, at the sale month), so scrapping re-books NOTHING —

017226: // Tax-neutral by construction (proven: phase0_scrap_test.js). Reason 'joblot'

017228: function _canScrap(i){
017229:   if(!i||i.scrappedAt)return false;
017230:   if(_activeJobLotMembership(i.id))return false;
017231:   if((i.item||'').trim().toUpperCase()==='MONTH END')return false;
017232:   // Removable unless it's currently sold/resold AND not back in your hands. isReturned always
017233:   // wins (a returned item is on hand regardless of prior sale/resale or an odd leftover state).
017234:   return !((i.dateSold||i.resaleSalePrice)&&!i.isReturned);
017235: }

020161: function _trapDialogTab(e,modal){
020162:   const focusable=Array.from(modal.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),[href],input:not([disabled]),select:not([disabled]),textarea:not([disabled]),[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')).filter(function(x){return x.offsetParent!==null;});

020165:   if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){e.preventDefault();last.focus();}
020166:   else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){e.preventDefault();first.focus();}
020167: }

020176: // v1.4.6 — Canonical lifecycle state machine. All primary item actions use
020177: // this single resolver so List / Mark Sold / Return / Relist do not drift apart.
020178: function _itemLifecycleState(i){
020179:   if(!i)return 'missing';
020180:   if(i.scrappedAt)return 'disposed';
020181:   if(i.isReturned)return 'returned';
020182:   if(i.resaleSalePrice||i.dateSold)return 'sold';
020183:   if(i.state==='sourced')return 'sourced';
020184:   return 'listed';
020185: }
020186: function _lifecycleCan(i,action){
020187:   const s=_itemLifecycleState(i);
020188:   if(action==='list')return s==='sourced';
020189:   if(action==='markSold')return s==='listed';
020190:   if(action==='return')return s==='sold';
020191:   if(action==='relist')return s==='returned';
020192:   if(action==='moveUnlisted')return s==='returned';
020193:   return false;
020194: }
020195: function _lifecycleActionSummary(i){
020196:   const s=_itemLifecycleState(i);
020197:   return {state:s,list:_lifecycleCan(i,'list'),markSold:_lifecycleCan(i,'markSold'),returnItem:_lifecycleCan(i,'return'),relist:_lifecycleCan(i,'relist'),moveUnlisted:_lifecycleCan(i,'moveUnlisted')};
020198: }

020200: function markSold(m,id){
020201:   if(_jobLotActionGuard(id))return;
020202:   const item=(DB[m]||[]).find(i=>i.id===id);
020203:   if(!item)return;
020204:   if(!_lifecycleCan(item,'markSold')){toast('Mark Sold is only available for an active listed item.','error');return;}
020205:   const hasFullReturn=(item.returnHistory||[]).some(r=>r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay');
020206:   const today=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
020207: 
020208:   if(hasFullReturn){
020209:     // Resale flow — price/shipping/promo already set during relist, just need date
020210:     MODAL_CALLBACK=function(date,price){
020211:       item.resaleDateSold=date;
020212:       item.resaleSalePrice=price||item.salePrice||0;
020213:       item.resaleShippingCost=item.shippingCost||0;
020214:       // Freeze Sale 2 buyer-paid postage at the moment the resale completes.
020215:       // Previously receipts/P&L fell back to live i.postage, so editing the
020216:       // returned item later could rewrite historical Sale 2 economics.
020217:       item.resalePostage=item.postage||0;
020218:       // E3 P1 #3 — Carry sale 2 packaging cost from i.packagingCost into
020219:       // the resale-specific field so calcGrossProfit's resale block can
020220:       // subtract it. confirmRelist now updates i.packagingCost to the user-
020221:       // specified sale 2 value, so this captures the right number.
020222:       item.resalePackagingCost=item.packagingCost||0;
020223:       item.resalePromoPercent=item.promoPercent||0;
020224:       item.isReturned=false;
020225:       item.state='sold';
020226:       item.grossProfit=calcGrossProfit(item);
020227:       saveDB();

021202:     const r=i.returnHistory[k];
021203:     if(r.type==='full_seller'||r.type==='full_ebay'){idx=k;break;}

021332: function undoRelist(m,id,rtnIdx){
021333:   const i=(DB[m]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
021334:   if(!i||!i.returnHistory)return;
021335:   const r=i.returnHistory[rtnIdx];
021336:   if(!r||!r._relistedAt)return;
021337:   const saleNo=Math.max(1,Number(r.saleNo)||1);
021338: 
021339:   // A relist can only be undone while it is still the latest structural step.
021340:   // If the next Sale-N already completed, step that sale back first.
021341:   const _liveNo=i.resaleSalePrice?_currentResaleSaleNo(i):null;
021342:   const _laterFrozen=(i.returnHistory||[]).some(function(r2){
021343:     return r2!==r&&(r2.type==='full_seller'||r2.type==='full_ebay')&&Math.max(1,Number(r2.saleNo)||1)>saleNo;
021344:   });
021345:   if((_liveNo&&_liveNo>saleNo)||_laterFrozen){
021346:     toast('Undo the later sale first, then undo this relist.','error');
021347:     return;
021348:   }
021349: 
021350:   // Remove relist markers from this return entry. The listing fee belongs to
021351:   // the relist action itself, so a correction that undoes the relist removes
021352:   // that fee from the event ledger as well.
021353:   const _undoneDate=r._relistedAt;
021354:   delete r._relistedAt;
021355:   delete r._relistCondition;
021356:   delete r._platformAtRelist;
021357:   delete r._listingFeeAtRelist;
021358: 
021359:   // v2.15.0 — drop the paired maintenance entry, or the relist counter and the
021360:   // freshness clock would keep counting a relist that has just been undone.
021361:   if(Array.isArray(i.refreshHistory)){
021362:     const _paired=i.refreshHistory
021363:       .map(function(e,ix){return {e:e,ix:ix};})
021364:       .filter(function(o){return o.e.type==='relist'&&o.e._fromReturn&&o.e.date===_undoneDate;})
021365:       .pop();

021544:   }

## app.js 21899-21963
021899: 
021900: 
021901: 
021902: 
021903: // ── KPI deltas (period-over-period) ────────────────────────────────────────
021904: // Maps the active period to its previous equivalent, runs the SAME calc over
021905: // that window, and returns % change per headline KPI. Powers the dashboard
021906: // "↑ vs last period" chips. Returns null per-metric when a baseline isn't
021907: // meaningful (no prior data, or all-time view), so the UI can hide the chip.
021908: 
021909: // Convert a YYYY-MM-DD date string to its month key, e.g. '2026-04-23' → 'APR-26'.
021910: // Returns null for empty/invalid input. Used to bucket sale events by sale month
021911: // (rather than the listing-month bucket the item lives in inside DB).
021912: 
021913: // Build a list of sale events that landed in month `m` (sale1 + sale2, attributed
021914: // by dateSold / resaleDateSold respectively). Each entry is a SALE EVENT — an item
021915: // with both an original sale and a resale in different months will produce two
021916: // entries, one per month.
021917: //
021918: // Returns array of: {item, month (DB key the item lives in), sale ('1' or '2'),
021919: //                    saleDate, salePrice, profit, roi, daysToSell, isReturned}
021920: //
021921: // `sale1` profit/revenue uses the item's original-sale-only state — for items
021922: // that have been relisted, this means reconstructing the original sale via the
021923: // snapshot stored on the full_return entry (see _origSalePrice convention).
021924: // `sale2` uses calcGrossProfit on the full item (which already includes the
021925: // relist accounting).
021926: // Return/refund events are dated independently from the original sale.
021927: // This preserves the original sale in its sale month and records the cash impact
021928: // in the month the return/refund was actually logged.
021929: 
021930: // ── F2/F3 event-profit reconciliation ──────────────────────────────────────
021931: // The sale-1, return and sale-2 events for one item must sum to
021932: // calcGrossProfit(i) — the canonical per-item all-time gross that feeds the
021933: // Dashboard, Money and cash. Historically the sale-2 event carried the WHOLE
021934: // item's gross (which already includes the sale-1 leg + the returns), so a
021935: // resold-after-return item double-counted; separately a fully-returned or
021936: // partially-refunded item's events missed the promoted-listing boost credit
021937: // and the partial-refund fee credit that calcGrossProfit applies. Fix: give
021938: // each event a BASE contribution (sale-1 = clean snapshot gross, return = its
021939: // own cash impact, sale-2 = 0) and route the whole residual onto the item's
021940: // TERMINAL event (sale-2 if resold, else the latest-dated return, else sale-1).
021941: // The set then sums to calcGrossProfit(i) by construction, for every shape.
021942: // DISPLAY-ONLY: the SA103/tax path (_buildPnLSummary → _saleBreakdown) reads
021943: // per-leg breakdowns and never reads these .profit fields.
021944: 
021945: 
021946: // v2.21.15 — The resale (sale 2) valued on its OWN leg: resale price with the
021947: // resale's shipping/packaging/promo and the item cost SUNK (0, paid in cycle 1).
021948: // Mirrors _sale1EventSnapshot. calcGrossProfit(this) == the item page's Sale 2
021949: // net, so the resale month shows honest resale economics.
021950: 
021951: // Fix B (B1): the Sales-History screen derives its month universe from item
021952: // STORAGE keys (purchase months). But events are attributed by SALE/RETURN
021953: // date, so any sale, resale or return dated in a month no purchase touches was
021954: // dropped from the all-time sum. This returns the set of 'MMM-YY' keys that
021955: // actually hold an event, so callers can widen their universe to cover them.
021956: 
021957: 
021958: // ─── DATE-RANGE STATEMENT HELPERS ────────────────────────────────────────
021959: // Sale events landing in an arbitrary [from,to] date window (inclusive).
021960: // Mirrors getSaleEventsInMonth's relist/return handling but filters by date.
021961: 
021962: // Sum of mileage + business expenses in [from,to]. Returns {trips,expenses,total,rows}
021963: // where rows is an array of expense line rows for embedding into a statement.

022599: // E4 P2 — Inline sale 1 snapshot edit. Mirrors ipSaveNum pattern but writes
022600: // to the full-return entry's _*AtRelist fields via applyRelistSnapshot rather
022601: // than to the live i.* fields. Used by the "Sale 1 details" expander on the

022607:   if(isNaN(v))return;

022609:   // expects a decimal (0.05) for that field, raw £ for the others.
022610:   const values={};
022611:   if(field==='shipping')values.shipping=v;
022612:   else if(field==='postage')values.postage=v;
022613:   else if(field==='packaging')values.packaging=v;
022614:   else if(field==='promoPercent')values.promoPercent=v/100;
022615:   else if(field==='salePrice')values.salePrice=v;
022616:   else return;
022617:   if(!applyRelistSnapshot(m,id,values))return;
022618:   saveDB();
022619:   const s=_getSecStates();renderItemPage(m,id);_applySecStates(s);
022620: }
